CO-Calib constructs initialization-friendly anchors, co-visible multi-camera constraints, and coverage-completion frames without changing the existing calibration workflow or optimization backend. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real multi-fisheye systems demonstrate that CO-Calib improves the overall success rate from 68.1% to 99.3%, increases extrinsic accuracy, and augments real-world calibration stability. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/HKUST-Aerial-Robotics/CO-Calib.

</details>


### [109] [FORGE: Towards Functional Tool-Use Generalization via Keypoint Trajectory Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05780)
*Chuhao Zhou,Liquan Wang,Shuxin Cao,Xiangyu Chen,Yuxuan Hu,Boyu Ma,Animesh Garg,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FORGE框架，将功能泛化解耦为功能推理（预测关键点轨迹）和动作执行两个阶段，在七种工具的击打任务基准上，对未见工具的模拟和真实世界成功率提升超2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能轻松将书本、石头、鞋子等任意物体当作锤子使用，但机器人训练在特定工具上后无法将相同功能迁移到新颖工具上。这种功能泛化鸿沟源于：工具间共享视觉可识别的功能意图，但每个工具需要完全不同的运动模式，现有方法无法桥接这种视觉-动作空间的差异。

Method: 提出FORGE两阶段策略：(1) 功能推理阶段：从无动作数据中预测泛化的关键点轨迹（对比了affordance图像、人类视频提示、2D关键点轨迹，发现关键点轨迹最佳）；(2) 动作执行阶段：将预测的关键点轨迹通过少量演示数据ground到具体机器人动作上。

Result: 在七种工具的击打功能基准上，FORGE在模拟和真实环境中均一致优于SOTA方法，对未见工具的平均成功率提升超过2倍。

Conclusion: 关键点轨迹作为中间表示能够最好地平衡功能表达性和动作可接地性；将功能推理与动作执行解耦的两阶段策略能有效实现机器人的功能泛化。

Abstract: While humans readily repurpose a book, a stone, or a shoe to drive a nail, robots trained on specific tools fail to transfer the same function to novel ones -- a gap we formalize as functional generalization. Such tools share a common functional intent that is visually recognizable, yet this perceptual similarity does not carry over to action space, where each tool demands an entirely different motor pattern. To bridge this gap, we explore intermediate representations including affordance images, human video prompts, and 2D keypoint trajectories, finding that keypoint trajectories best balance functional expressiveness and action groundability. Building on this, we propose FunctiOnal Reasoning and Grounded Execution (FORGE), a two-stage policy that decouples functional reasoning from action execution: predicting generalizable keypoint trajectories from action-free data, then grounding them into robot actions with limited demonstrations. On a seven-tool hitting-function benchmark, FORGE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on unseen tools in both simulation and the real world, achieving over 2X improvement in average success rate.

</details>


### [110] [GraspIT: A Dataset Bridging the Sim-to-Real gap and back for Validated Grasping SE(3) Pose Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05869)
*Paul Koch. Adem Karakurt,André Sers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspIT提供大规模逼真RGB-D抓取数据集，包含1035个仿真场景和100个真实场景、约31.6万帧带标注的RGB-D图像，通过物理滑移测试生成连续质量分数和轨迹可达性检查，并支持Real↔Sim闭环标注回传，所有工具开源且Docker化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取数据集无法同时提供逼真RGB-D观测、物理验证的抓取质量标注以及仿真与真实世界之间的原则性桥梁，GraspIT旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中构建桌面场景，通过四阶段物理滑移测试对Franka Panda实例的抓取候选进行标注，生成轨迹可达性检查和连续质量评分（超出力封闭）。Real↔Sim循环将仿真标注回投到100个真实场景。提供实例掩码、6-DoF位姿、物理属性和评分抓取。

Result: 约230万个候选抓取中，83%通过为good（s≥0.50），17%通过力封闭但滑移测试失败的作为分级难负样本。发布约31.6万帧RGB-D数据（1035仿真+100真实场景）。

Conclusion: GraspIT提供了大规模、物理验证的仿真+真实抓取数据集，支持抓取策略学习和行为克隆，且工具完全开源。

Abstract: Robust robotic grasping of novel objects requires datasets that simultaneously provide photorealistic RGB-D observations, physically validated grasp quality annotations, and a principled bridge between simulation and the real world, which existing datasets lack to provide jointly. \textbf{GraspIT} addresses this gap: tabletop scenes in NVIDIA Isaac Sim are annotated via a four-stage physical slip-test on parallel Franka Panda instances, producing trajectory-reachability checks and continuous quality scores beyond force-closure.Of ${\sim}$2.3M candidates, 83% pass as \emph{good} ($s{\geq}0.50$); the 17% that passed force-closure but failed the slip-test provide graded hard negatives. A Real$\leftrightarrow$Sim loop back-projects these labels onto 100 real-world scenes. The release provides ${\sim}$316k annotated RGBD frame sets across 1035 sim and 100 real scenes, with instance masks, 6-DoF poses, physical object properties, and scored 6-DoF grasps. All tools are open-source and Docker-containerized. The trajectory planning within Isaac Sim further allows streaming of high resolution demonstrations for tabletop manipulation policy learning and behavior cloning.

</details>


### [111] [DexTele: A Dual-Arm Dexterous Teleoperation System Based on Motion Retargeting and Adaptive Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05883)
*Yuanchuan Lai,Qing Gao,Ziyan Liang,Xianfeng Cheng,Junjie Hu,Zhaojie Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了DexTele双臂灵巧遥操作系统，结合基于视觉的运动重定向和自适应力控制，实现跨平台运动映射和柔顺抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧遥操作中，机器人架构异构性和抓取物体多样性导致精确运动重定向和柔顺抓取困难。

Method: 1) 基于视觉的运动重定向模块：使用运动图编码器和潜在优化实现跨平台精确映射；2) 自适应抓取模块：结合VLM和MPC预测抓取力并进行梯度在线优化。

Result: 在多个机器人平台上实现精确的运动重定向和柔顺抓取，具备跨平台泛化能力。

Conclusion: DexTele通过视觉运动重定向和VLM+MPC自适应抓取，解决了双臂遥操作的跨平台泛化和交互性问题。

Abstract: In dual-arm dexterous teleoperation, cross-platform generalization of motion retargeting and interactivity of grasping are crucial. However, the heterogeneity of robotic architectures and the wide variety of grasping objects pose significant challenges to achieving precise motion retargeting and compliant grasping in dual-arm dexterous teleoperation. To address these challenges, a dual-arm dexterous teleoperation system (DexTele) is proposed based on motion retargeting and adaptive force control. First, a vision-based motion retargeting module is designed to generate preliminary robot motions from human images. In this module, a motion-graph encoder and latent optimization are proposed for precise and convenient cross-platform motion retargeting. Second, an adaptive grasping module is designed to achieve compliant grasping. This module combines a vision-language model (VLM) with model predictive control (MPC), allowing the system to predict the required grasping force for a target object and perform gradient-based online optimization. Finally, extensive experiments demonstrate that the DexTele achieves precise motion retargeting and compliant grasping with generalization across multiple robot platforms.

</details>


### [112] [Intercepting an Agile Target with Net-Carrying Drones using Competitive Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05939)
*Timothée Gavin,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出使用多架携带捕捉网的敏捷无人机拦截单架敏捷无人机，将问题建模为竞争性多智能体强化学习（MARL）任务，采用MAPPO+优先虚拟自我对弈（PFSP）训练追捕者和逃逸者，在低层控制指令（CTBR）下实现敏捷飞行，相比启发式基线在捕获率、捕获时间和碰撞率上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对敏捷无人机拦截任务中对手策略不断变化导致的非平稳性和灾难性遗忘问题，需要一种能够适应不同对手策略的鲁棒策略。

Method: 将问题建模为竞争性MARL，使用MAPPO结合PFSP训练追捕者和逃逸者，在基于CTBR低层控制指令的高保真模拟器中实现敏捷飞行。

Result: 在捕获率、捕获时间和碰撞率上优于启发式基线；消融实验表明PFSP提升了策略鲁棒性，低层控制命令对学习有效策略至关重要。

Conclusion: 方法能有效解决敏捷无人机拦截问题，追捕者涌现出合作战术，证明了PFSP和低层控制命令在追逃任务中的重要性。

Abstract: This article presents a solution to intercept an agile drone by a team of agile drone carrying catching nets. We formulate the problem as a competitive Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) task. To address the problem of nonstationarity and catastrophic forgetting of agents overfitting to the current opponent strategy, we train the pursuers and the evader using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) with Prioritized Fictitious Self Play (PFSP). We train the agents in a high-fidelity simulator using low-level control commands, collective thrust and body rates (CTBR), to achieve agile flights for both the pursuers and the evader. We compare the performance of the trained policies in terms of catch rate, time to catch and crash rates, against heuristic baselines and show that our solution outperforms them. Ablation studies show that PFSP lead to more robust policies that can adapt to different opponent strategies, and that a low-level control commands are crucial for learning performing strategies in the pursuit-evasion task. Finally, a qualitative analysis of the learned behaviours highlights the emergence of cooperative tactics among the pursuers.

</details>


### [113] [Delay-Aware Active Triangulation with Uncertainty-Driven Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Counter-UAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05957)
*Seungwook Lee,David Hyunchul Shim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种延迟感知、不确定性驱动的多智能体强化学习框架，用于反无人机场景中的目标三维定位。通过Age-of-Information增强观测实现延迟感知协调，感知一致性奖励优于特权干净状态奖励（RMSE 0.547m vs 0.633m），多源协方差传播比仅考虑角噪声RMSE恶化2.8倍。MAPPO在4096并行环境中达到78.1%三角测量有效性，MLP策略仅0.7%，证明循环记忆对延迟补偿至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体主动视觉三角测量方法假设瞬时状态反馈，忽略了检测、通信和决策传播带来的累积延迟，导致实际部署中定位精度和鲁棒性下降。

Method: (1) Dec-POMDP + AoI增强观测实现延迟感知协调；(2) 感知一致性奖励设计；(3) 多源分析协方差传播（像素、位姿、云台、内参不确定性）；使用MAPPO在4096并行环境训练。

Result: MAPPO策略：0.547±0.217m RMSE，78.1%三角测量有效性；AoI提升三角测量有效性10.6个百分点；感知一致性奖励比特权干净奖励RMSE降14%，丢失率降27%；仅角噪声导致RMSE恶化2.8倍；MLP策略仅0.7%有效性。

Conclusion: 延迟感知和不确定性建模对多智能体主动视觉定位至关重要，循环记忆是补偿延迟的必要组件，感知一致性奖励提供了稳定性-鲁棒性权衡的更优解。

Abstract: Multi-agent active visual triangulation enables precise 3D localization of aerial targets by coordinating mobile observers with controllable cameras. However, existing methods assume instantaneous state feedback, ignoring cumulative latency from detection, communication, and decision propagation. We present a delay-aware, uncertainty-driven multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for target localization in Counter-UAS applications. Our contributions are: (1) a Dec-POMDP formulation with Age-of-Information (AoI) augmented observations enabling delay-aware coordination -- AoI improves triangulation validity by 10.6 percentage points; (2) a controlled comparison showing that perception-consistent rewards outperform privileged clean-state rewards (0.547 m vs.0.633 m RMSE, 27% fewer track losses) -- both policies are trained through identical observation noise but differ in what they are optimized for, producing a stability-robustness tradeoff; and (3) multi-source analytical covariance propagation incorporating pixel, pose, gimbal, and intrinsics uncertainties -- restricting to angular noise alone causes 2.8-fold RMSE degradation. Experiments with MAPPO in 4096 parallel environments achieve 0.547 +- 0.217 m RMSE with 78.1% triangulation validity, while MLP policies achieve near-zero validity (0.7%), confirming recurrent memory as essential for delay compensation.

</details>


### [114] [Imagined Rollouts are Kinematic, Not Dynamic: A Diagnosis of Long-Horizon World-Model Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05966)
*Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Korbinian Moller,Yuan Gao,Christian Oefinger,Sebastian Schmidt,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"运动学vs动力学"重框架解释世界模型的长程失败，并设计运动学一致性误差（iKCE）诊断指标，在DreamerV3上验证模型倾向于运动学想象而非动力学想象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上世界模型的长程失败被笼统归因于累积误差，但未能区分误差类型。本文旨在精确诊断世界模型失败的本质是"运动学式"而非"动力学式"想象。

Method: 提出运动学一致性误差（iKCE）作为每步诊断指标，衡量 rollout 偏离封闭形式运动学零假设的程度；配合扰动协议测试物理条件跨域边界时 iKCE 是否响应。在 DMC walker-walk 上使用 DreamerV3 检查点实例化。

Result: iKCE在想象rollout中比匹配真实物理的rollout高约两个数量级；在摩擦系数扫描跨越步态崩塌边界时，iKCE保持统计平坦，而策略奖励崩塌，呈现运动学而非动力学特征。

Conclusion: 该诊断能在大于步态周期的时间尺度上区分运动学与动力学想象，揭示世界模型的核心缺陷在于缺乏动力学因果推理。

Abstract: Long-horizon failure in world models is conventionally attributed to compounding error, a generic framing that does not distinguish what kind of error compounds. We propose a kinematic-vs-dynamic reframing: world models tend to imagine kinematically rather than dynamically. We operationalize this as the imagined Kinematic-Consistency Error, a per-step diagnostic that measures how far a rollout departs from a closed-form kinematic null, paired with a perturbation protocol that tests whether iKCE responds when physical conditions cross a regime boundary. We instantiate the diagnostic on a released DreamerV3 checkpoint trained on DMC walker-walk, where imagined iKCE runs roughly two orders of magnitude above that of matched real-physics rollouts. Across a friction sweep that crosses the gait-collapse boundary, the model's iKCE stays statistically flat even as the trained policy's reward collapses through the same range, providing the kinematic-not-dynamic signature. The diagnostic distinguishes kinematic from dynamic imagination at horizons longer than the embodiment's gait period.

</details>


### [115] [RoboTALES: Learning Reasoning-Guided Robot Policies via Task-Aligned Simulated Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06018)
*Hanan Gani,Tejal Kulkarni,Madhoolika Chodavarapu,Nicklas Hansen,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboTALES是一个单阶段框架，利用预训练视频生成模型学习任务对齐的模拟未来帧，并通过层次化LLM规划器分解子目标、VLM批评者提供奖励反馈，从而训练机器人策略，在长 horizon 操作任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频生成模型作为视觉运动控制骨干时，生成的未来帧常偏离任务意图且不可靠地以动作为条件，导致难以用于规划或策略提取。

Method: (1) 层次化LLM规划器将复杂任务分解为子目标序列，引导视频生成模型的想象；(2) VLM批评者评估生成的未来帧，通过奖励反馈使模型内部表示聚焦于目标，实现单阶段任务对齐模拟未来帧学习与策略训练。

Result: 在RoboCasa和LIBERO10的多类操作任务上，RoboTALES一致优于现有方法，尤其长 horizon 任务提升显著。

Conclusion: 通过将视频生成器锚定于抽象推理，可产生时间一致的 rollout 和更连贯的动作，有效解决视频生成模型用于控制时的漂移问题。

Abstract: Pretrained video generative models are promising backbones for visuomotor control, but their imagined futures often drift from task intent and are not reliably action-conditional. As a result, these models can be difficult to use for planning or policy extraction. To address these limitations, we propose RoboTALES, a single-stage framework that learns task-aligned simulated futures and uses them to train robot policies. Our approach introduces two key innovations: (1) a hierarchical LLM-based planner that breaks complex tasks into a sequence of subgoals to guide the model's imagination; and (2) a VLM-based critic that evaluates these ``imagined'' futures and uses reward-based feedback to keep the model's internal representations focused on the goal. By anchoring the video generator in abstract reasoning, we produce temporally consistent rollouts and more coherent actions. We evaluate RoboTALES on diverse manipulation tasks from RoboCasa and LIBERO10, and show that our method consistently outperforms existing methods, especially in long-horizon tasks. Our code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/hananshafi/RoboTALES.

</details>


### [116] [ThorArena: Benchmarking Humanoid Physical Interaction with Human Motion-Force Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06052)
*Chenhao Yu,Hongwu Wang,Weitao Zhang,Youhao Hu,Jiachen Zhang,Gangyang Li,Alois Knoll,Shaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ThorArena基准，基于真实人机交互数据（同步运动+双手力测量）评估人形机器人全身控制策略在接触式任务中的力感知性能，发现传统无外力评估无法揭示的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只关注运动学跟踪，忽略交互力对跟踪精度、稳定性和鲁棒性的影响，缺乏力感知评估标准。

Method: 采集六种物理交互任务中同步全身运动与双手力真实数据；提出力感知跟踪分数(FATS)等评估指标；在仿真中重放记录交互力，提供统一评估接口。

Result: 力感知评估揭示了传统无外力评估下隐藏的显著性能差异，证明该基准能有效区分不同控制策略在接触任务中的表现。

Conclusion: ThorArena为力感知人形机器人交互提供了可复现的评估框架，填补了接触式行为基准的空白。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly expected to perform contact-rich tasks that require not only accurate whole-body motion but also robust physical interaction with surrounding objects and humans. Although recent advances in humanoid motion imitation and whole-body control have achieved remarkable tracking performance, existing datasets and benchmarks primarily focus on kinematic motion while largely overlooking synchronized interaction forces. As a result, current evaluations fail to capture how external interaction forces affect tracking accuracy, stability, and control robustness.
  In this paper, we present ThorArena, a benchmark for evaluating force-aware humanoid interaction based on human demonstrations with synchronized motion and force measurements. We collect a real-world interaction dataset that simultaneously captures whole-body human motion and forces exerted by both hands across six representative physical interaction tasks. Based on these demonstrations, we propose force-aware evaluation metrics that jointly assess whole-body tracking accuracy, robustness under different force levels, control effort, and episode survival through the Force-Aware Tracking Score (FATS) and complementary diagnostic metrics. We further establish a unified benchmark protocol that replays recorded interaction forces in simulation and provides a standardized evaluation interface for different humanoid control policies.
  Experiments on representative whole-body control policies demonstrate that force-aware evaluation reveals substantial performance differences that remain largely hidden under conventional no-force evaluation. ThorArena provides a practical and reproducible framework for studying force-aware humanoid interaction and offers a new benchmark for evaluating contact-rich humanoid behaviors.

</details>


### [117] [MP-MPPI: A Motion Primitive Guided Sampling-Based Optimizer for Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06123)
*Marlon Mathisen,Aksel Vaaler,Olav Egeland,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将运动基元与MPPI方法结合的方法，通过在采样优化循环中评估运动基元和扰动控制序列，增强趋近全局最优解的收敛性，提升采样控制器路径规划能力。在四旋翼模拟器上验证了障碍物导航任务，改善了控制空间探索同时保持实时控制所需的快速响应特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型模型预测控制（如MPPI）虽然速度快、适合实时控制，但路径规划能力有限，容易陷入局部最优。本文旨在通过引入运动基元的结构化采样来增强全局搜索能力，弥补采样控制器的路径规划不足。

Method: 在MPPI的实时采样优化循环中，同时评估预定义的运动基元（结构化采样）和传统随机扰动控制序列，将两者合并进行加权更新，从而在保持实时性的同时增强控制空间探索。

Result: 在四旋翼模拟器的障碍物场导航任务中，所提方法相比标准MPPI在路径规划成功率和收敛质量上均有提升，且保持了实时控制所需的快速响应。

Conclusion: 将运动基元注入MPPI采样过程是一种有效的实时控制空间探索增强手段，能够在保持快速反应能力的同时改善路径规划的全局最优性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel method that extends the Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) method with motion primitives for additional structured sampling, which enhances the convergence towards a globally optimal solution. By evaluating motion primitives and perturbed control sequences in a real-time sampling-based optimization loop, this work addresses the limitations of the path planning capabilities of sampling-based controllers. The algorithm is implemented on a quadcopter simulator and tested on an obstacle field navigation task. It is demonstrated that the proposed approach enhances exploration of the control space while maintaining the fast, reactive behavior required for real-time control.

</details>


### [118] [EAGOR: Embodied Reasoning in Omni-direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06165)
*Shriram Damodaran,Soumyaratna Debnath,Yan Wu,Wei-Yun Yau,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EAGOR提出了一种无需训练、几何感知的框架，将360°方向推理建模为球面上的递归贝叶斯估计，通过球谐信念场（SH-BF）在代理运动下等变传播方向信念，显著提升地图无关导航的方向估计一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM将360°观测投影为ERP图像后使用透视图像架构处理，忽略了球面几何特性（像素代表观测方向），导致代理运动引起的视角变换下方向估计不一致，尤其影响地图无关导航等任务。

Method: 将方向推理建模为球面上的递归贝叶斯估计，提出球谐信念场（SH-BF），利用球谐函数的全局旋转感知基在球流形上进行方向估计，无需训练骨干VLM，在运动下等变传播信念。

Result: 在HOS和OSR-Bench上分别取得+34.4%和+45.6%的相对增益；导航成功率+14.6%，步数减少17.7%，平均角度误差降低24.5%。

Conclusion: EAGOR通过几何感知的球面贝叶斯推理框架，无需额外训练即可显著提升360°方向推理的鲁棒性和一致性，在仿真和真实机器人实验均有效。

Abstract: Omni-directional (360°) cameras provide embodied agents with a holistic view of their surroundings, making them suited for directional reasoning in tasks such as navigation and object search. Existing Vision Language Models (VLMs) project 360° observations to 2D equirectangular projection (ERP) images and process them using architectures designed for perspective images. However, they ignore the spherical nature of 360° observations, where each pixel represents a viewing direction relative to the agent. Consequently, their direction estimates often become inconsistent under camera view transformations caused by agent motion. This limitation is particularly critical for map-free navigation, where the agent must continuously estimate the target direction in its egocentric frame. We propose EAGOR, a training-free, geometry-aware framework for embodied 360° directional reasoning. Instead of predicting target directions as ERP image coordinates, EAGOR formulates directional reasoning as recursive Bayesian estimation directly on the sphere. It maintains a continuous belief over target directions and propagates it equivariantly under agent motion without training the backbone VLMs. To achieve this, we introduce the Spherical Harmonic Belief Field (SH-BF), whose spherical harmonic representation provides a globally defined, rotation-aware basis for directional estimation on the spherical manifold. This formulation eliminates ERP seam discontinuities, latitude distortions, and interpolation errors. We evaluate EAGOR on two benchmark datasets and real-world experiments with a legged robot across directional reasoning tasks. EAGOR consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving average relative gains of +34.4% and +45.6% on HOS and OSR-Bench, respectively, while improving navigation success by +14.6%, reducing step count by 17.7%, and lowering mean angular error by 24.5%.

</details>


### [119] [Calf-Integrated Arms for Bimanual Quadruped Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06186)
*Yan Pan,Yuanchuan Ren,Chipui Chan,Jingcheng Sun,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将双臂集成到四足机器人前腿小腿上的设计（每个前腿包含棱柱滑块+两个旋转关节+夹爪），使机器人能在四足站立时进行双臂地面操作，无需后腿站立；单臂搬运时另一侧可行走。使用VLM在技能边界处自动切换技能，实现长时域自主操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人操作设计在操作能力与站立稳定性之间存在权衡：躯干安装单臂、抬腿操作、或后腿站立才能双手操作，均限制了实际应用中的稳定性和灵活性。

Method: 将每个前腿小腿改造为包含棱柱滑块+两个旋转关节+夹爪的机械臂结构，使前腿既承担行走又承担操作功能；使用VLM（视觉语言模型）在技能边界处基于头部相机图像和任务状态自动选择预定义库中的技能，实现长时域自主任务。

Result: 在仿真中完成了三个双手操作任务：长时域柜子任务（自主技能选择）、协同双手提举、臂间交接。

Conclusion: 该设计使得四足机器人能在四足着地状态下进行双手地面操作，同时保持行走能力，有效解决了操作与站立的权衡问题。

Abstract: Most quadruped loco-manipulation designs trade manipulation capability against stance. A trunk-mounted arm sits high and usually carries a single arm; using the legs as manipulators lifts the manipulating leg off the ground; and even leg-mounted grippers reach two-handed tasks only by rearing onto the hind legs. This paper integrates a manipulator with a prismatic slider, two revolute joints, and a gripper into each front calf of a Unitree Go2. The two arms grasp objects at ground level and manipulate with both hands while all four feet stay planted, without rearing. With one arm carrying, the base stays free to walk. A vision-language model sequences skills from a predefined library at each skill boundary, conditioned on the head-camera image and task state, for long-horizon autonomy. In simulation, the design performs three bimanual tasks: a long-horizon cabinet task under autonomous skill selection, a cooperative two-handed lift, and an inter-arm handover.

</details>


### [120] [APVI-SLAM: Real-Time Acoustic-Pressure-Visual-Inertial Localization and Photorealistic Mapping System in Complex Underwater Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06222)
*Hanwen Zhang,Yipeng Zhu,Xiaopeng Guo,Huajian Huang,Sai-Kit Yeung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: APVI-SLAM是一个面向极端水下环境的实时多传感器融合SLAM系统，结合视觉、IMU、DVL和压力计，实现鲁棒的定位与高保真建图，并提出四叉树引导的3D高斯建图模块和珊瑚礁多模态数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端水下环境中视觉特征退化严重，导致传统视觉-惯性SLAM估计器发散；尽管DVL和压力计可提供辅助约束，但视觉失效时的鲁棒多传感器融合仍是难题，同时缺乏水下建图质量的评测基准。

Method: 提出可靠性感知定位框架（动态重加权传感器估计器 + 滑动窗口冻结恢复策略），以及四叉树引导的增量式水介质建模与3D高斯优化建图模块，并贡献了同步多模态珊瑚礁 surveying 数据集。

Result: 在公开和自建基准上，APVI-SLAM在定位精度和重建质量上均达到 state-of-the-art，且支持实时运行。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效提升了水下视觉失效时的SLAM鲁棒性，并首次实现实时高保真水下建图，填补了水下建图评测空白。

Abstract: Extreme subsea environments often cause severe feature de-gradation and estimator divergence in underwater visual-inertial SLAM. Although sensors like Doppler Velocity Logs (DVL) and pressure gauges provide auxiliary constraints, robust multi-sensor fusion during intermittent visual failure remains challenging. To address this, we present APVI-SLAM, a real-time multi-sensor fusion SLAM system that achieves both accurate underwater localization and photorealistic mapping. Our approach introduces a reliability-aware localization framework that dynamically reweights sensor estimators and employs a sliding-window freezing strategy to recover from tracking failures, substantially enhancing system robustness. Furthermore, for high-fidelity scenes reconstruction, we propose an efficient quadtree-guided mapping module that facilitates incremental water-medium modeling and 3D Gaussian optimization. Recognizing the lack of benchmark for underwater mapping evaluation, we also contribute a coral reef surveying dataset with synchronized multi-modality data. Extensive experiments on public and our proposed benchmarks demonstrate that APVI-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art localization and reconstruction quality at real-time speeds.

</details>


### [121] [RoboVAST: Automated Scenario-Based Validation of Robots at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06248)
*Frederik Pasch,Samuel Wiest,Argentina Ortega,Nico Hochgeschwender*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于场景组合建模的机器人系统验证方法论，并实现了RoboVAST框架，支持声明式测试规范、插件式场景生成和可扩展容器化执行，在5个室内地图上构建了5480个场景配置、超过10万次执行的数据集，总计1800+小时仿真和1873公里导航，通过20次重复执行区分系统故障与随机异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统验证的关键依赖于测试工况，但场景选择与变体生成目前依赖人工经验，难以规模化，损害可复现性和验证结论的可信度。

Method: 提出组合式场景建模方法，形式化描述场景维度的变化、实例化、执行与解释；构建RoboVAST框架，包含声明式测试规范、插件式场景生成、可扩展容器化执行与集成结果分析。

Result: 在5个室内地图、5480个场景配置、超过10万次执行、1800+小时仿真、1873公里导航的实验规模下验证了该框架的可行性，20次重复执行可区分系统故障与随机异常。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展、可复现的机器人系统验证方法论与框架，通过大规模系统性实验证明了其有效性。

Abstract: Validation of robotic systems critically depends on the operating conditions under which they are assessed. Scenario selection and variation are often manual, experience-driven, and difficult to scale, which harms reproducibility and weakens validation conclusions. We propose a scenario-based methodology that models scenarios compositionally and formalizes how these dimensions are varied, instantiated, executed, and interpreted. Building on this, we introduce RoboVAST, a framework that realizes declarative campaign specifications, plugin-based scenario generation, and scalable containerized execution with integrated result analysis. We demonstrate the approach with a navigation dataset comprising 5480 scenario configurations and over 100000 execution runs across five indoor maps with varied paths, sensor noise, software parameters, and obstacle settings, totaling more than 1800 hours of simulated operation and 1873 km traveled. Twenty repetitions per configuration allow us to distinguish systematic failures from stochastic anomalies.

</details>


### [122] [Diagnosing Semantic Handoff Failures in Agent-Orchestrated Vision-Language-Action Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06256)
*Ke Rui,Yushen Zuo,Jiawei Wang,Haoran Jia,Jinming Ma,Weitao Zhou,Minglei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过BEHAVIOR-1K基准研究了机器人长时域家务任务中技能间语义交接（semantic handoff）问题，构建了智能体编排的视觉-语言-动作执行框架，利用多视角VLM验证技能执行状态。实验表明，单技能在干净初始状态下成功率77-100%，但在链式组合中频繁失败，揭示了单技能成功与长时域任务可靠完成之间的显著差距，指出未来VLA技能库必须学习对杂乱链式状态分布的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域家务任务需要机器人组合多个语言条件技能，但连续技能之间的边界往往不明确——一个技能完成自身后置条件后，可能使机器人、物体或相机视角处于下一技能无法可靠启动的状态。现有方法多关注单技能成功率，忽略技能间语义交接导致的组合失败问题。

Method: 构建智能体编排的VLA执行框架：调用基于π₀.₅的技能检查点（从清洗后的BEHAVIOR-1K演示训练），为每个技能分配类型化参数和步数预算，使用多视角VLM验证决定执行推进、重试或重规划。通过两种初始状态分布（干净技能边界快照 vs. 链式终端状态）对比评估相同检查点，分离单技能能力与长时域组合鲁棒性。

Result: 选取的导航、抓取、放置和开门技能在干净快照下成功率77-100%（人工审核VLM验证），但链式组合滚动频繁因链式状态而停滞。失败轨迹归因于下一技能就绪状态、目标定位和底层控制执行问题，揭示了单技能成功与可靠长时域任务完成之间的巨大差距。

Conclusion: 将近乎零的端到端任务成功转化为可操作的诊断指标，表明未来VLA技能库必须学习对杂乱链式状态分布的鲁棒性——这种分布在干净演示中系统性欠表征。

Abstract: Long-horizon household tasks require robots to compose many language-conditioned skills, yet the boundary between consecutive skills is rarely explicit. A skill may satisfy its own postcondition while leaving the robot, objects, or camera views in a state from which the next skill cannot reliably start. We study this semantic handoff problem in BEHAVIOR-1K through an agent-orchestrated vision-language-action execution harness. The harness invokes $π_{0.5}$-based skill checkpoints trained from cleaned BEHAVIOR-1K demonstrations, assigns each skill typed arguments and a step budget, and uses multi-view vision-language model verification to decide whether execution should advance, retry, or replan. To separate isolated skill competence from long-horizon compositional robustness, we evaluate the same checkpoints under two initial-state distributions: clean skill-boundary snapshots and chained terminal states produced by previous skills. Selected navigation, grasping, placement, and door-opening skills achieve 77--100% success from clean snapshots under human-reviewed verification, yet composed rollouts still frequently stall from chained states. Execution traces attribute these failures to next-skill readiness, target grounding, and low-level control execution, revealing a substantial gap between single-skill success and reliable long-horizon task completion. These findings turn near-zero end-to-end task success into actionable diagnostics, showing that future VLA skill libraries must learn robustness to the messy chained-state distribution that clean demonstrations systematically underrepresent.

</details>


### [123] [Optimal Transport Q-Learning for Flow Policy Steering and Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06262)
*Andreas Sochopoulos,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Nikolaos Tsagkas,João Moura,Michael Gienger,Sethu Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出最优传输Q学习(OTQL)，通过优势加权条件最优传输流匹配，用少量交互(50-60 episodes)对次优flow policy进行RL后训练微调与加速，使单任务策略成功率从36%提升至86%，VLA从38%提升至76%，同时减少70%推理步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散和流策略在机器人VLA模型中表现优异，但高质量演示数据难以获取，次优策略在分布偏移下表现不佳；同时需要快速推理。缺乏一种无需昂贵蒸馏、仅靠少量机器人经验就能微调和加速次优flow policy的方法。

Method: 提出OTQL (Optimal Transport Q-Learning)，核心是优势加权条件最优传输流匹配(advantage weighted conditional optimal transport flow matching)，结合RL后训练，在仅50-60个episode的交互预算内微调flow policy并加速推理。

Result: 单任务策略成功率从36%提升至86%；预训练VLA策略从38%提升至76%；每个动作生成的推理步数减少70%。在仿真和真实机器人任务中均有效，无需计算昂贵的蒸馏。

Conclusion: OTQL利用机器人自身经验对flow policy进行后训练，能以极少的交互显著提升策略成功率和推理效率，是一种实用的VLA/foundation model微调替代方案。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow policies have recently demonstrated remarkable performance in robotic applications by accurately capturing multimodal robot trajectory distributions, especially in the context of vision language action (VLA) models. However, high quality policy performance also requires fast inference and high quality demonstrations, which are often hard to get. Lack of these leads to suboptimal policy behaviors and failure under distribution shifts. In this work we address the problem of fine-tuning and accelerating suboptimal flow-based policies using the robot's experience through RL post-training. We introduce Optimal Transport Q-Learning (OTQL), a new method for finetuning flow policies using advantage weighted conditional optimal transport flow matching. OTQL can finetune and accelerate flows with an interaction budget of 50-60 episodes while avoiding computationally expensive distillation in simulation and real-world robot tasks. Our results show that OTQL post-trains flow policies using the robot's own experience, increasing average success percentage of single-task policies from 36% to 86% and of a pre-trained VLA from 38% to 76% while reducing the number of inference steps per action generation by 70%.

</details>


### [124] [LAMP: Latent Motion Prior-Guided Real-World Learning for Dexterous Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06323)
*Xinye Yang,Zhiyuan Ma,Hongze Yu,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang,Xiaojie Chai,Xinlei Chen,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于潜在运动先验（Latent Motion Prior）的灵巧手真实世界学习框架，将高维手部动作压缩到历史条件潜空间，通过三阶段流程（先验预训练、视觉运动策略训练、在线残差RL微调）在仅需少量示范的情况下实现高达98.75%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维灵巧手动作空间导致模仿学习误差放大、强化学习探索易破坏接触，现有方法在原始手部动作空间中无约束探索样本效率低且对物理硬件有风险，亟需一种既能利用示范知识又能安全高效在线优化的方法。

Method: 1) 提出潜在运动先验模块（Latent Motion Prior），将近期手部动作历史编码为紧凑的潜变量，并解码为可执行的高维手部目标；2) 三阶段框架：从示范数据预训练先验 → 训练预测原生手臂指令+潜手部偏移的视觉运动策略 → 在同一潜手部空间进行在线残差RL微调。

Result: 在4个真实机器人灵巧操作任务上，初始IL成功率达56.25%，在线RL后提升至98.75%，其中3个任务达100%，1个任务达95%。

Conclusion: 将高维手部动作压缩到低维潜空间，能够有效引导探索靠近示范的接触一致性运动，大幅提升真实世界灵巧手学习的样本效率和安全性。

Abstract: Real-world learning for dexterous hands remains brittle because high-dimensional hand actions amplify imitation errors and make reinforcement-learning exploration prone to contact-breaking motion. While combining imitation learning (IL) with online reinforcement learning (RL) can reduce manual supervision, unconstrained exploration in raw hand-action spaces is sample-inefficient and risky for physical hardware. We introduce a latent motion prior module (\prior{}) that maps recent hand-action histories to a compact, history-conditioned latent prior and decodes continuous latent commands into executable high-dimensional hand targets. Built on this prior, \method{} is a three-stage real-world dexterous learning framework: it pretrains \prior{} from demonstrations, trains a visuomotor policy that predicts native arm commands and latent hand-action offsets, and improves the policy with online residual RL in the same latent hand-action space. This shared, decodable interface lets residual exploration make local corrections near demonstrated, contact-consistent hand motions rather than perturbing every finger joint independently. We evaluate \method{} on four real-robot dexterous manipulation tasks against raw, linear, and discrete hand-action interfaces. Starting from small task-specific demonstration sets, \method{} achieves a 56.25\% average IL success rate and raises it to 98.75\% after online RL, reaching 100\% final success on three tasks and 95\% on the remaining task.

</details>


### [125] [OrchardBench: A Physically-Grounded, GPU-Parallel Apple-Orchard Simulation Benchmark for Agricultural Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06337)
*Humphrey Munn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OrchardBench，一个基于 Newton 引擎、GPU 并行的苹果园物理仿真器。树木由随机 L-系统生长，枝干为符合欧拉-伯努利梁理论的扭转弹簧阻尼器，苹果通过茎绳连接并可在特定拉力下脱落。移动机械臂+腕部深度相机闭环，支持领域随机化，在笔记本 GPU 上可实时运行多个并行环境。定义收获完整性、吞吐量、植物损伤指标，基线方法约收获可触及水果的 1/8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人水果采摘的田间实验成本高、不可重复（果园一年仅可用数周、每棵树不同、控制错误会永久损伤作物），现有图形学/农学树模型缺乏物理交互，机器人学习的 GPU 并行仿真器中缺少合理的树木模型。

Method: 基于 Newton 引擎构建 GPU 并行仿真，树木由随机 L-系统生长并实例化为完整刚体；枝干用扭转弹簧阻尼器建模刚度（依据 Euler-Bernoulli 梁理论），可断裂为自由铰链；苹果由茎绳连接，在文献支撑的拉力下脱落；可移动机械臂+腕部深度相机实现感知-采摘闭环；每环境领域随机化生成不同树木。

Result: 基线方法在检测到的水果上成功约 40%，实际收获仅约树上可触及水果的 1/8，留下显著提升空间；在笔记本 GPU 上支持多环境实时运行。

Conclusion: OrchardBench 为农业机器人采摘提供了首个物理真实、GPU 并行的仿真基准，填补了现有仿真器中缺乏合理树木模型的空白，为新型自主采摘方法提供了测试平台。

Abstract: Robotic tree-fruit harvesting is a flagship problem for agricultural automation, but progress is bottlenecked by the cost and irreproducibility of field experiments: an orchard is available only weeks a year, every tree is different, and a control error can permanently damage the crop or the plant. The tree models used in graphics and agronomy are geometrically detailed but physically inert, while the GPU-parallel simulators used in robot learning contain no plausible trees. We present OrchardBench, a physically-grounded, GPU-parallel simulation of apple-orchard trees on the Newton engine. Each tree is grown by a stochastic L-system and instantiated as a fully articulated body: branches are compliant torsional spring-dampers whose stiffness follows Euler-Bernoulli beam theory, they break at a wood modulus of rupture and fall as free hinges, and apples are independent bodies on stem tethers that detach at literature-grounded pull forces and load the branch when pulled. A moving, density-controllable foliage layer occludes the canopy as real leaves do. Every physical parameter is tied to a published source. Per-environment domain randomization makes each batched world a distinct tree, and a mobile manipulator with a wrist depth camera closes the loop with geometric fruit perception and an autonomous harvesting baseline. Careful engineering of the solver and the model lets OrchardBench run many parallel environments at interactive rates on a laptop GPU. We define the tasks and a metric suite spanning harvest completeness, throughput, and plant damage (with a per-canopy-zone breakdown), and report baseline results across foliage, fruit load, terrain, canopy zone, and parallelism. The analytic baseline succeeds on about 40% of the fruit it detects and harvests only about an eighth of the reachable fruit on a tree, leaving clear headroom for novel autonomy approaches.

</details>


### [126] [Responsible Personalisation: The Double-Edged Sword of Personalisation in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06344)
*Antonio Andriella,Jauwairia Nasir,Andrea Rezzani,Alyssa Kubota,Dimitri Lacroix,Tamlin Love,Aniol Civit,Vicky Charisi,Elisabeth Andre,Wing-Yue Geoffrey Louie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于生命周期和情境感知的个性化人机交互框架，从具身视角系统分析了个性化过程中的伦理风险（自主性侵蚀、偏见建模、操纵、去人性化、隐私侵犯），并给出设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于负责任个性化的文献零散，缺乏对伦理风险如何在交互情境中产生和演变的系统性理解；尤其在HRI中，机器人的具身性和社会存在会放大或产生新型风险。

Method: 提出生命周期-情境感知框架，将个性化过程的阶段（收集、建模、决策、执行、反馈）与交互特征（短期/长期、开放域/封闭域）结合，系统分析伦理风险的涌现机制。

Result: 识别并分析了五种关键伦理风险在不同交互情境下的表现，转化为可操作的设计建议，并指出了开放性研究挑战。

Conclusion: 该框架为个性化机器人行为提供了更系统、透明且符合伦理的设计基础，有助于规范化个性化HRI的风险管理。

Abstract: While personalisation is becoming a defining capability in human-robot interaction (HRI), the existing literature on responsible personalisation remains fragmented, offering isolated accounts of ethical risks without a structured understanding of how they emerge across interaction contexts. This gap is particularly critical in HRI, where robots' embodiment and social presence can amplify and reshape such risks or generate new types of risks. We present a lifecycle-based and context-sensitive framework for personalised HRI, grounded in an embodiment-aware perspective. The framework combines stages of the personalisation process with interaction characteristics (short-term vs. long-term, open-domain vs. closed-domain), enabling systematic analysis of how risks arise and evolve. Building on this, we conduct an integrative analysis of key ethical risks, including autonomy erosion, biased user modelling, manipulation, dehumanisation, and privacy violations, and examine how they manifest across contexts. We translate these insights into actionable design recommendations and outline open research challenges. By structuring both the design space and risk landscape of personalised HRI, this work provides a foundation for more systematic, transparent, and ethically grounded approaches to personalised robot behaviour.

</details>


### [127] [Training-Free Acceleration for Vision-Language-Action Models with Action Caching and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06370)
*Ryuji Oi,Hikari Otsuka,Kosuke Matsushima,Yuki Ichikawa,Masato Motomura,Tatsuya Kaneko,Daichi Fujiki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ActionCache，一种即插即用的外部缓存机制，通过复用历史中间动作来加速流匹配VLA模型的迭代去噪推理过程，在π0.5和GR00T-N1.6上分别实现11.75倍和34.43倍的推理加速，且保持高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA模型因迭代去噪过程带来巨大计算瓶颈，限制了实时部署。为解决这一关键挑战，需要在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下大幅降低推理延迟。

Method: 提出ActionCache即插即用外部缓存，存储带紧凑多模态键的中间动作，通过从相似历史上下文（跨不同轮次甚至不同任务）检索来热启动目标动作附近的生成，从而减少去噪步数。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境实验中，对π0.5实现11.75倍加速，对GR00T-N1.6实现34.43倍加速，任务成功率保持高位。

Conclusion: ActionCache能够在不显著降低任务成功率的情况下，大幅加速流匹配VLA模型的推理，是一种高效且普适的加速方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for generalizable robotic manipulations. In particular, flow matching-based VLA models have shown remarkable success due to their capability to generate precise and smooth action sequences and capture multimodal distributions. However, the iterative denoising process in the action head acts as a major computational bottleneck, posing a critical challenge for real-time deployment. To address this challenge, we propose ActionCache, a plug-and-play external cache that opportunistically reuses past intermediate actions to warm-start generations from the vicinity of target actions, thereby drastically reducing the inference latency. Specifically, ActionCache stores the intermediate actions with compact multimodal keys, which enables retrieval from similar past contexts across different episodes or even different tasks. Experimental results in simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that ActionCache maintains high task success rates in a low-latency regime, achieving inference acceleration of up to $11.75\times$ and $34.43\times$ for representative flow-based VLA models, $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T-N1.6, respectively.

</details>


### [128] [Towards Real-World Applications with an Autonomous Powered Wheelchair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06383)
*Simone Arreghini,Alessandro Giusti,Alex Bordini,Enrico Ferrara,Giovanni Fulgoni,Antonio Paolillo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成自主感知、手势交互和导航的自平衡电动轮椅原型系统，在商用Genny Zero平台上整合RGB-D相机和LiDAR，实现了轮椅远程呼叫和人员跟随两项辅助功能，验证了自主机器人与辅助移动平台结合的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电动轮椅自主性有限，缺乏与先进感知和导航能力的有效集成，无法在复杂真实环境中为轮椅使用者提供主动、自适应且直观的辅助移动支持。

Method: 基于商用自平衡轮椅Genny Zero（通过重心偏移实现免手操作），集成RGB-D相机用于人感知和手势交互，LiDAR用于定位和导航，实现轮椅呼叫和人员跟随两项功能，并进行了受限室内导航验证。

Result: 成功展示了原型系统的两项辅助应用（远程呼叫和跟随），验证了集成方案的可行性，同时指出了通往用户就绪方案的主要技术挑战。

Conclusion: 初步研究表明自主机器人与辅助移动平台的结合具有潜力，但距离真正可用、易用、智能的移动解决方案仍有关键技术挑战需要解决。

Abstract: Wheelchair users call for assistive mobility systems that provide active support, adapt to dynamic environments, and are intuitive and user-friendly. However, powered wheelchairs typically still provide limited autonomy and lack effective integration with advanced perception and navigation capabilities, particularly in complex real-world environments. This paper presents a preliminary study toward autonomous powered wheelchairs for real-world assistive mobility. We introduce a proof-of-concept prototype that integrates autonomous perception, gesture-based interaction, and navigation on a commercially available self-balancing powered wheelchair. The proposed system builds upon Genny Zero, a commercial self-balancing wheelchair that enables hands-free and intuitive operation through body-weight shifting. To extend its capabilities toward autonomous operation, we integrate an RGB-D camera for human-aware perception and interaction, together with a LiDAR sensor for localization and navigation. We demonstrate the integrated system in two assistive applications: (i) hailing, allowing users to call the wheelchair from a distance; and (ii) people-following, where the wheelchair follows a person using leader-follower strategies, including a constrained indoor navigation example. The results highlight the potential of combining autonomous robotics with assistive mobility platforms, while also showing the feasibility of the proposed integration and identifying the main technical challenges that must be addressed before moving toward user-ready, accessible, and intelligent mobility solutions. A video demonstrating the experimental setup and results is available at: https://youtu.be/LVAix_Qx7bM.

</details>


### [129] [Learning to Throw Objects Safely in Multi-Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06388)
*Mohammadreza Kasaei,Klemen Voncina,Hamidreza Kasaei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种势场状态表示（Potential Field Representation, PFR），将目标篮子的吸引力和障碍物的排斥力紧凑地编码到固定尺寸网格上，结合强化学习（SAC最优）实现机器人在杂乱环境中绕过障碍物精准投掷物体，仿真和真实实验均达到90%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人投掷方法（如TossingBot）假设无障碍物环境。本文致力于解决在障碍物随机分布的真实杂乱场景中，机器人如何投掷物体至目标篮子的问题，填补了可靠投掷在杂乱环境中研究的空白。

Method: 提出势场状态表示（PFR），将目标吸引和障碍排斥编码到固定尺寸网格，使RL策略可泛化至任意数量和配置的障碍物。策略从示教初始化，在仿真中用SAC/DDPG/TD3优化，其中SAC表现最稳定。在真实机器人上实现sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在杂乱场景中真实机器人投掷成功率高达90%；PFR相比显式状态编码在成功率和泛化到未见障碍物配置方面均更优；SAC三者中最一致；对未见过的投掷物体具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 势场状态表示为非结构化环境中安全高效的机器人投掷提供了实用且鲁棒的表示方案，展示了良好的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Robotic throwing enables fast and efficient object placement beyond the robot's immediate workspace, but reliable throwing in cluttered environments remains underexplored. Existing approaches, such as TossingBot, learn throwing strategies from visual input but assume obstacle-free settings. In this paper, we address the problem of throwing objects into a target basket while avoiding obstacles placed randomly in the scene. We introduce a potential field state representation that compactly encodes both basket attraction and obstacle repulsion on a fixed-size grid, enabling reinforcement learning (RL) policies to generalize across arbitrary numbers and configurations of obstacles. The policy is initialized from kinesthetic demonstrations and optimized in simulation using three state-of-the-art RL algorithms (SAC, DDPG, TD3). Among these, SAC achieves the most consistent performance across scenarios. We compare the potential field representation against explicit state encodings and demonstrate that it achieves higher success rates and better scalability to unseen obstacle configurations. Real-robot experiments with unseen throwable objects confirm robust sim-to-real transfer, achieving up to $90\%$ success in cluttered scenes. These results demonstrate that PFR provides a practical and robust representation for safe and efficient robotic throwing in unstructured environments. A video showcasing our experiments is available at: https://youtu.be/ZZnJf8ua2dE

</details>


### [130] [From Foundation to Application: Improving VLA Models in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06403)
*Wei Wu,Fangjing Wang,Fan Lu,He Sun,Shi Liu,Yunnan Wang,Yibin Yan,Yong Wang,Shuailei Ma,Xinyang Wang,Yibin Liu,Shuai Yang,Tianxiang Zhou,Kejia Zhang,Lei Zhou,Cheng Su,Nan Xue,Bin Tan,Han Zhang,Youchao Zhang,Fei Liao,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Kecheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LingBot-VLA 2.0 通过改进数据处理（6万小时预训练数据）、扩展动作空间（全身自由度）和引入预测动态建模（视频+深度先验），提升了VLA在跨任务、跨本体和长时域操作上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室条件与真实世界应用之间的差距阻碍了VLA基础模型的实际部署，现有模型在跨任务、跨本体泛化以及复杂长时域操作上能力不足。

Method: (1) 数据处理pipeline改进，收集6万小时数据（5万小时20种机器人轨迹+1万小时第一人称人类视频）用于预训练；(2) 扩展动作空间至头、腰、移动底盘、灵巧手等全身自由度；(3) 将未来预测作为代理任务，利用视频表示模型（语义先验）和深度估计模型（几何线索）进行预测动态建模。

Result: 在GM-100基准上的通用设置评估验证了各改进的有效性，扩展的全身自由度预训练数据使LingBot-VLA 2.0在两个机器人平台上展现出强大的跨本体长时域移动操作能力。

Conclusion: 通过数据规模扩展、动作空间扩展和预测动态建模三个维度的改进，有效缩小了VLA模型从实验室到实际应用的距离。

Abstract: Despite recent progress of VLA foundation models, the disparity between laboratory conditions and real-world applications continues to impede their practical implementation. To bridge this gap, we present LingBot-VLA 2.0, which advances LingBot-VLA through improvements in three functional domains. (1) Generalization across tasks and embodiments. Compared to the previous version, we revamp the data processing pipeline and curate around 60,000 hours of data for pretraining, including 50,000 hours of robot trajectories spanning 20 robot configurations and 10,000 hours of egocentric human videos. (2) Expanded action space in addition to dual-arm hardware platforms. In particular, our system accommodates degrees of freedom for the heads, waists, mobile bases, and dexterous hands, thereby empowering the robots to tackle more complex tasks in practical scenarios. (3) Predictive dynamics modeling for improved temporal reasoning. Specifically, we formulate future prediction as a proxy task, facilitated by a video representation model for semantic priors and a depth estimation model for geometric cues. Evaluations on the GM-100 benchmark, conducted in a generalist setting, validate the beneficial impact of these proposed modifications. Furthermore, benefiting from the expanded pretraining data that covers whole-body degrees of freedom, LingBot-VLA-2.0 demonstrates strong cross-embodiment long-horizon mobile manipulation capability across the two robotic platforms.

</details>


### [131] [WristMimic: Full-Body Humanoid Control with Wrist-Guided Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06438)
*Wongyun Yu,Youngwoon Kim,Minsu Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WristMimic框架，将无接触身体运动与接触丰富的手部操作解耦：身体和手腕由运动学姿态引导，手指不直接监督而是由物体跟踪和接触结果学习；手腕作为两域间的自然门控，配合专用重置约束和奖励优先级，实现跨手部形态的指关节无关重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接跟踪完整手部姿态会过度约束接触丰富的手指行为，且位置轨迹无法指定操作所需的接触力，导致物理仿真中人体物体交互重定向困难。

Method: 1) 将全身控制分解为无接触域（身体+手腕监督运动学姿态）和接触丰富域（手指由物体跟踪和接触结果学习）；2) 手腕作为两域之间的自然门控；3) 引入手腕专用重置约束和奖励优先级确保可靠放置。

Result: 匹配或超越使用完整手指姿态监督的方法，同时实现跨多种手部形态的指关节无关重定向。

Conclusion: 手腕引导的全身控制框架有效解耦了接触丰富的手指操作与无接触的身体运动，消除对手指姿态直接监督的依赖，提升了物理仿真中操作重定向的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Retargeting human object interaction demonstrations to physics based simulation requires reproducing not only body motion but also the object motion and contacts that make manipulation succeed. However, position only hand trajectories do not specify the contact forces needed to manipulate objects, and directly tracking them can overconstrain contact rich finger behavior. We introduce WristMimic, a wrist guided whole body control framework that explicitly separates contact free body motion from contact rich hand manipulation. The contact free body and wrist are guided by kinematic pose targets, whereas the fingers are not directly supervised by human hand pose. Instead, they learn grasping and manipulation behaviors from object tracking and contact outcomes. Our key insight is that the wrist is the natural gate between these two regimes. It is largely free from contact and can be tracked kinematically, yet it determines the global hand configuration and places the fingers within reachable grasp affordances. To ensure reliable wrist placement during interaction, we introduce wrist specific reset constraints and reward prioritization. Experiments show that WristMimic matches or surpasses methods using full finger pose supervision while enabling finger agnostic retargeting across diverse hand embodiments.

</details>


### [132] [SIEVE: Structure-Aware Data Selection for Imitation Learning with VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06442)
*Changti Wu,Bin Yu,Zhaolong Shen,Shijie Lian,Xiaopeng Lin,Cong Huang,Zhirui Zhang,Lei Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIEVE，一种结构感知的数据筛选方法，通过发现视觉运动基元和转换接口，在VLA模仿学习中选择高复用性的演示数据，仅用50%数据和50%训练步数即可超越全量训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器人演示数据集存在冗余、噪声和覆盖不均的问题，现有数据筛选方法仅在轨迹或状态-动作层面评估，忽略了构成长程行为的可复用结构，导致更多数据不一定带来更好的策略。

Method: 1) 从分割轨迹中发现视觉运动基元；2) 通过最大化复用感知的结构暴露度（在边际收益递减下），为组合模式分配选择预算；3) 在每个组合模式桶内选择medoid轨迹，保留中心、稳定且利于模仿的演示。

Result: 在多个数据集、基准和VLA模型上，SIEVE始终优于竞争性数据筛选基线；仅用50%演示和50%训练步数即可超越全量训练。

Conclusion: 通过基元和转换捕获的可复用结构是高效VLA模仿学习的重要信号，结构感知的数据筛选能显著提升数据效率和策略性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically trained by imitation learning on large-scale robot demonstration datasets, but more data does not necessarily yield better policies due to redundancy, noise, and uneven coverage. Existing data selection methods often assess demonstrations at either the trajectory or state-action level, missing the reusable structures that compose long-horizon behaviors. In this paper, we propose SIEVE, a structure-aware data selection method for VLA imitation learning. SIEVE views demonstrations as compositions of reusable primitives and transition interfaces. It first discovers visuo-motor primitives from segmented trajectories, then allocates selection budgets to composition patterns by maximizing reuse-aware structural exposure under diminishing returns. Finally, it selects medoid trajectories within each composition-pattern bucket to retain central, stable, and imitation-friendly demonstrations. Experiments across multiple datasets, benchmarks, and VLA models show that SIEVE consistently outperforms competitive data selection baselines. Notably, SIEVE can surpass full-data training while using only 50% of demonstrations and 50% of training steps, suggesting that reusable structure, captured through primitives and transitions, is an important signal for efficient VLA imitation learning.

</details>


### [133] [Hilti-Trimble-Oxford Dataset: 360 Visual-Inertial Benchmark with Floor Plan Priors for SLAM and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06464)
*Samuele Centanni,Yuhao Zhang,Yifu Tao,Julien Kindle,Frank Neuhaus,Tilman Koß,Aryaman Patel,Michael Helmberger,Emilia Szymańska,Torben Gräber,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在活跃建筑工地采集的视觉-惯性SLAM与定位数据集，包含7层楼、8个月、30个序列，并附带基于LiDAR-IMU的 ground truth 轨迹。同时报告了一项开放挑战的评测结果：62支队伍参与SLAM，22支参与平面图定位，定位误差更高，表明该任务仍需持续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本360相机+IMU替代昂贵LiDAR用于建筑工地进度监控，需要高精度视觉SLAM和平面图定位系统，但缺乏反映真实工地挑战（光照变化、运动工人、快速运动、重复结构）的公开数据集来推动研究。

Method: 在8个月的建筑施工期内，使用360相机+IMU采集7层楼的视觉-惯性序列，并用刚性连接的LiDAR-IMU高精度SLAM系统提供真值轨迹。基于此数据集举办了公开SLAM和定位挑战赛。

Result: 数据集包含30个序列；SLAM挑战吸引62支队伍，定位挑战仅22支；定位误差显著高于SLAM误差，凸显了建筑环境定位的难度。

Conclusion: 该数据集与基准可用于支持建筑工地自动化监控中的SLAM与定位研究，尤其是高难度定位任务的进一步发展。

Abstract: Automated progress monitoring on construction sites is an active area of research and development. Robot and human-carried mapping systems have been developed to build 3D maps of building and infrastructure projects. While LiDAR-based mapping systems achieve high accuracy, the cost of LiDAR can be prohibitive. Consumer-grade cameras with wide field of view ("360 cameras") combined with embedded inertial measurement units (IMUs) provide a cost-effective alternative. To support change detection and progress monitoring, highly accurate visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) and floor plan-referenced localization systems are required. In this paper we present a high-quality dataset collected at an active construction site, which captures realistic challenges such as variable lighting conditions, moving workers, fast motions, and repetitive structures. The dataset offers thirty visual-inertial sequences recorded across seven floors over an eight-month period of the construction project. Ground truth trajectories were collected using a high quality LiDAR-inertial SLAM system rigidly attached to the 360 camera. Additionally, we report the results of an open research challenge evaluating the best visual SLAM and localization systems from around the world. The Challenge attracted substantially higher participation in SLAM, with 62 teams compared to 22 in floor-plan-referenced localization, reflecting the broader maturity of SLAM methods. The higher errors in localization further highlight the difficulty of this task in construction and point to the need for continued research, which this dataset is intended to support. The dataset and the benchmark are publicly available at: https://hilti-trimble-challenge.com/dataset-2026.

</details>


### [134] [Clustering-Embedded Model Predictive Path Integral Control: Avoiding Averaging-Induced Failure and Enabling Efficient Cluster Selection for Dynamic Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06499)
*Zidong Liu,Kaixin Chang,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CE-MPPI通过嵌入DBSCAN聚类和几何方向特征，解决标准MPPI在非凸环境中因平均化导致的碰撞与犹豫问题，在2D仿真和UR5e机械臂上实现48%时间减少和12%路径缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPPI在杂乱环境中因重要性加权更新会将不相容的轨迹平均化，导致障碍物前的"犹豫"甚至碰撞，需要从根本上解决这一架构性缺陷。

Method: 在MPPI采样和前向仿真之间嵌入DBSCAN聚类+碰撞衍生几何方向特征提取，分离可行轨迹模式与不可行噪声，并通过智能选择逻辑在静态场景优化最小代价、动态场景主动避障。

Result: 2D JAX仿真消除了障碍物前犹豫；UR5e在Isaac Gym中CUDA并行rollout实现48%时间节省和12%路径缩短。

Conclusion: CE-MPPI通过聚类重构控制律，从架构上解决了MPPI的固有问题，在实机上验证了有效性。

Abstract: With the widespread availability of parallel computing hardware, sampling-based motion planning methods such as Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control have become increasingly powerful for complex nonlinear systems in non-smooth task spaces. However, the sampling and forward-simulation pipeline in MPPI suffers from averaging-induced failure in cluttered environments, where the importance-weighted update averages incompatible rollouts and leads to hesitation or even collision when an obstacle lies directly ahead. This paper proposes Clustering-Embedded MPPI (CE-MPPI), a framework that architecturally resolves the averaging-induced failures inherent in standard MPPI within non-convex environments. Rather than simply mitigating interference, CE-MPPI redefines the control law by integrating a high-fidelity pruning and clustering stage. By leveraging density-based spatial clustering of applications with noise (DBSCAN) alongside a novel geometric direction feature that is extracted from collision-derived reference points, the system isolates feasible trajectory modes from the noise of infeasible rollouts. This is paired with an intelligent selection logic that optimizes for minimum cost in static scenes while actively steering opposite to obstacle flux in dynamic environments. Experiments in 2-D JAX-accelerated simulations show that CE-MPPI alleviates obstacle-front hesitation and avoids persistent coupling with moving obstacles in dynamic scenes. In particular, real-world tests on a 6-DoF UR5e manipulator with CUDA-parallel rollouts in Isaac Gym achieve a 48\% reduction in time-to-goal and a 12\% shorter end-effector path.

</details>


### [135] [Hypothesis-driven Model Expansion under Uncertainty for Open-World Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06501)
*Anxing Xiao,Hanbo Zhang,Tianrun Hu,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向开放世界的机器人规划框架，利用基础模型生成假设并通过自动化规划进行验证，实现机器人自主知识扩展与任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统闭环规划方法依赖预编程知识库，在面对未知环境和任务时无法自主扩展知识，限制了服务机器人在人类环境中的实用部署。

Method: 提出开放世界规划框架，结合基础模型生成关于状态和转移的初始假设，通过自动化规划产生同时验证假设和执行任务的行动序列，迭代执行并利用基础模型的验证反馈来精炼和扩展知识。

Result: 在模拟和真实环境中进行大量实验，证明该框架能够实现自主知识扩展并在开放世界中有效运行。

Conclusion: 将不确定性感知的模型扩展与基础模型和规划相结合，有助于推进家庭服务机器人的实际部署。

Abstract: We consider an open-world planning setting in which service robots must operate in unknown environments with incomplete knowledge of objects and actions. Traditional closed-world approaches with pre-programmed knowledge bases fail when robots encounter unexpected situations and tasks, posing a fundamental challenge for autonomous knowledge expansion in human environments. In this work, we propose an open-world planning framework that enables robots to automatically generate, verify, and update hypotheses about their abstract world models. Our key insight is to explicitly maintain uncertainty-aware knowledge expansion and integrate hypothesis verification into goal-reaching planning. The framework leverages foundation models to generate initial hypotheses over states and transitions, and applies automated planning to produce action sequences that jointly address hypothesis verification and task execution. Through iterative execution and refinement, the robot expands its knowledge by incorporating verification feedback from the foundation models when hypotheses prove incorrect. Extensive experiments in simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that our framework enables autonomous knowledge expansion and effective operation in open-world settings. These results indicate that integrating uncertainty-aware model expansion from robot foundation models with planning advances the practical deployment of household service robots.

</details>


### [136] [Neural-ESO: A Dual-Pathway Architecture for Provably Robust Learning-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06535)
*Fan Zhang,Richie Suganda,Jinfeng Chen,Wenhua Liu,Hantao Fu,Bin Hu,Qin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经扩展状态观测器（Neural-ESO）的学习型抗干扰控制框架，采用双路径架构（神经网络前馈预测 + 传统ESO校正），通过Lyapunov和小增益分析保证闭环误差一致最终有界，在强地面效应干扰的四旋翼降落任务中验证了精度-鲁棒性权衡和部署可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的控制方法在部署后过度依赖已学习的模型，缺乏对预测误差的鲁棒补偿机制，导致在外分布场景下性能退化甚至失效。

Method: 双路径架构：预测路径用神经网络提供前馈扰动估计加速收敛，校正路径用传统ESO补偿预测误差；对学习组件施加Lipschitz界约束，通过Lyapunov理论和小增益分析证明闭环稳定性。

Result: 在四旋翼降落强地面效应干扰任务中，相比SOTA基线，在正常和OOD场景下均展现出更优的精度-鲁棒性权衡和更高的训练、部署、迁移阶段的操作可靠性。

Conclusion: Neural-ESO有效结合了学习的加速能力和ESO的鲁棒补偿能力，为学习型抗干扰控制提供了一种可靠且实用的框架。

Abstract: A learning-enabled disturbance-rejection framework based on a Neural Extended State Observer (Neural-ESO) is presented in this letter. Unlike existing learning-based control methods that largely rely on the learned model once deployed, Neural-ESO adopts a dual-pathway architecture: a predictive pathway uses a neural network to provide a feedforward disturbance estimate that accelerates convergence, while a corrective pathway employs a conventional ESO to compensate prediction errors and prevent over-reliance on the neural component. Using Lyapunov theory and a small-gain analysis, we show that enforcing a Lipschitz bound on the learning component guarantees uniform ultimate boundedness of the closed-loop error dynamics. The proposed framework is validated on a quadrotor landing task subject to strong ground-effect disturbances across normal and out-of-distribution scenarios, demonstrating accuracy-robustness trade-off and greater operational reliability during training, deployment, and transfer compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [137] [UniLM-Nav: A Unified Framework for Zero-Shot Last-Mile Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06537)
*Zhuofan Zhang,Tianxu Wang,Guoxi Zhang,Yixiong Lin,Xilin Wang,Hongming Xu,Qing Li,Song-Chun Zhu,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLM-Nav是一个统一的零样本开放词汇末端导航框架，利用共享的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）后端，将末端导航分解为视角选择、任务条件化的可操作性接地和几何感知的基座姿态推理，在OVMM基准上超越之前SOTA方法MoTo 3.13个百分点，并在Unitree B2四足机器人+Z1机械臂上进行了真实世界验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 末端导航（到达目标附近后未保证操作就绪的基座姿态）是移动操作中的关键瓶颈，现有方法依赖手动姿态标注或特定任务训练，无法扩展到具有细粒度空间约束的开放词汇场景。

Method: UniLM-Nav将末端导航分解为三个子步骤：1）视角选择（从最近观测中选择最佳捕获目标的参考视图）；2）任务条件化的可操作性接地（在选定视图中接地任务相关的可操作点，并提升到机器人坐标系）；3）几何感知的基座姿态推理（基于接地可操作性点、任务上下文和机器人几何信息，推理出操作就绪的基座姿态）。所有步骤共享一个MLLM后端。

Result: 在OVMM基准上超越MoTo 3.13个百分点；消融实验验证各组件必要性；在Unitree B2四足机器人+6-DoF Z1机械臂上部署验证真实世界可行性。

Conclusion: UniLM-Nav通过MLLM统一处理视角选择、可操作性接地和几何推理，实现了无需训练、开放词汇的末端导航，有效提升了移动操作任务的成功率。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires a robot to navigate to a target object or receptacle and then perform intended manipulation. However, reaching the vicinity of the target does not guarantee a manipulation-ready base pose, a problem known as last-mile navigation. Prior methods for last-mile navigation either rely on manual pose annotation or task-specific training, limiting their scalability to open-vocabulary settings with fine-grained spatial constraints. We propose UniLM-Nav, a unified framework for zero-shot open-vocabulary last-mile navigation. UniLM-Nav decomposes last-mile navigation into view selection, task-conditioned affordance grounding, and geometry-aware base-pose reasoning, all resolved with a shared multimodal large language model (MLLM) backend. Specifically, UniLM-Nav first selects a reference view that best captures the target object or receptacle from recently collected observations. It then grounds task-relevant affordance point in the selected view and lifts the result into the robot-centric coordinate frame. Finally, conditioned on the grounded affordance, task context, and robot geometry, it infers a manipulation-ready base pose for the robot. We evaluate UniLM-Nav on the OVMM benchmark, where it outperforms the previous state-of-the-art method, MoTo, by 3.13 percentage points. Analyses show that the components of our method are crucial to final performance, and that the choice of MLLM also has a substantial effect. We further deploy UniLM-Nav on a Unitree B2 quadruped robot with a 6-DoF Unitree Z1 manipulator, validating its applicability to real-world mobile manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [138] [RynnWorld-Teleop: An Action-Conditioned World Model for Digital Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06558)
*Haoyu Zhao,Xingyue Zhao,Hangyu Li,Biao Gong,Kehan Li,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Deli Zhao,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出数字遥操作范式，通过生成式世界模型替代真实机器人，从单张参考图像合成高保真第一人称视频，实现与硬件无关的数据采集，并训练策略实现零样本Sim2Real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理遥操作需要操作员时间与特定硬件和工作空间绑定，成为机器人学习数据收集的瓶颈，亟需一种解耦物理约束的数据采集范式。

Method: RynnWorld-Teleop：深度感知骨骼条件化、渐进式人-机器人训练视频Diffusion Transformer、流式自回归蒸馏，将生成过程压缩为单次推理，实现40+ FPS实时交互生成。

Result: 纯生成数据训练的策略在灵巧双手任务上实现零样本Sim2Real；增强真实数据集持续提升成功率。

Conclusion: 数字遥操作作为高保真、可扩展的数据引擎，可替代物理遥操作，为下一代机器人智能体提供训练数据。

Abstract: Scaling robot learning requires massive, diverse trajectory data, yet collection is currently bottlenecked by physical teleoperation, where every demonstration binds operator time to specific hardware and workspaces. We introduce digital teleoperation, a paradigm that decouples data collection from physical constraints by replacing the real robot with a generative world model. In this framework, an operator's hand-pose stream drives a robot-centric generative world model to synthesize high-fidelity egocentric videos from a single reference image. The recorded pose stream serves as an embodiment-agnostic action label transferable to any target robot via standard retargeting, yielding complete state-action trajectories for imitation learning independent of physical hardware. We instantiate this paradigm in RynnWorld-Teleop, a system that integrates depth-aware skeletal conditioning, progressive human-to-robot training on a video Diffusion Transformer, and streaming autoregressive distillation. This pipeline compresses the generative process into a single-pass inference, enabling 40+ FPS, real-time interactive generation on a single H100 GPU. Policies trained exclusively on RynnWorld-Teleop-generated data achieve effective zero-shot Sim2Real transfer across dexterous and diverse bimanual tasks. Moreover, augmenting real-world datasets with our digitally teleoperated data consistently improves success rates, demonstrating that RynnWorld-Teleop serves as a high-fidelity, scalable data engine for the next generation of robotic agents.

</details>


### [139] [RynnWorld-4D: 4D Embodied World Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06559)
*Haoyu Zhao,Xingyue Zhao,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Deli Zhao,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RynnWorld-4D，一个从单张RGB-D图像和语言指令联合预测未来RGB、深度和光流的生成式世界模型，并在此基础上构建逆动力学策略头，实现灵巧双臂操控的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人世界模型多基于2D像素视频预测，与底层末端执行器动作之间的表征鸿沟较大。RGB、深度和光流的多模态协同能捕捉场景的4D动力学，更接近机器人动作空间，但缺乏统一的生成式建模方法。

Method: 1) RynnWorld-4D：三分支扩散架构，通过跨模态注意力与帧级3D RoPE联合生成RGB、深度、光流；2) Rynn4DDataset 1.0：2.54亿帧大规模伪标签数据集；3) RynnWorld-4D-Policy：逆动力学头，单次前向传播直接利用4D表征输出动作，无需多步去噪。

Result: 生成的4D预测在时空上具有一致性；在真实世界灵巧双臂操控任务上达到SOTA，特别在需要空间精度和时间协调的任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: RGB-DF多模态4D世界模型能有效弥合世界预测与策略学习之间的差距，为机器人操控提供物理有根据的表征空间。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in the open world requires not only recognizing what a scene looks like, but also anticipating how its 3D structure moves under interaction. We argue that synchronized RGB, depth, and optical flow, namely RGB-DF, provide a physically grounded representation that captures the underlying 4D dynamics of a scene. Compared to 2D pixel videos, this multi-modal synergy aligns visual appearance with geometric structure and temporal motion, creating a representation space significantly closer to the low-level end-effector actions demanded by robotic systems, thereby narrowing the gap between world prediction and policy learning. Building on this insight, we introduce RynnWorld-4D, a generative model that co-produces future RGB frames, depth maps, and optical flow from a single RGB-D image and a language instruction within one unified diffusion process. This 4D world model features a tri-branch architecture that integrates cross-modal attention with frame-wise 3D RoPE, ensuring that appearance, geometry, and motion evolve consistently. To supply training data at scale, we curate Rynn4DDataset 1.0, a massive dataset of over 254.4 million frames across egocentric human and robotic manipulation videos with high-quality pseudo-labels for depth and optical flow. We further propose RynnWorld-4D-Policy, an inverse dynamics head that consumes the internal 4D representations of RynnWorld-4D in a single forward pass, bypassing expensive multi-step denoising, to output robot actions in a closed-loop manner. Experiments show that RynnWorld-4D produces temporally and spatially coherent 4D predictions, and that RynnWorld-4D-Policy achieves state-of-the-art performance on real-world dexterous bimanual manipulation tasks, particularly excelling in tasks demanding spatial precision and temporal coordination.

</details>


### [140] [Embodied Human-Robot Interaction via Acoustics: A MARL Approach with AcoustoBots for Spatial Data Physicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06563)
*Shiqi Liu,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Prateek Mittal,Pengyuan Wei,Sriram Subramanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AcoustoBots是一种移动声悬浮数据物理化平台，TurtleBot3机器人搭载8×8超声相控阵，通过悬浮颗粒高度（1-10cm）编码城市标量数据（如人口密度），结合MADDPG多智能体强化学习实现碰撞感知导航，在4m×3m英国地图上实现单/双机器人任务成功率90%/80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据物理化通常是静态且脱离真实环境的，无法传达具身空间动态和用户参与感，需要一种移动的、能体现空间动态的物理数据展示方式。

Method: TurtleBot3机器人搭载8×8超声相控阵，声悬浮颗粒高度编码数据；MADDPG算法实现多智能体强化学习导航控制；GS-PAT声学控制器维持悬浮稳定性；PhaseSpace定位系统实现可重复的多机器人实验。

Result: 单机器人城市间遍历任务成功率90%，双机器人协同覆盖任务成功率80%，碰撞次数低，运动过程中实现稳定的声悬浮和位置依赖的高度渲染。

Conclusion: 声悬浮技术可作为简单、可一瞥即知的机器人介导通信线索，用于具身人机交互的空间数据分析。

Abstract: Traditional data physicalization is often static and disconnected from real environments, limiting its ability to convey embodied spatial dynamics and engage users. To address this limitation, we present AcoustoBots, a mobile acoustophoretic data-physicalization platform in which TurtleBot3 robots carry upward-facing 8 x 8 ultrasonic phased arrays. Each array levitates a particle whose height (1-10 cm) encodes a local urban scalar value, such as population density, noise, or traffic. A MARL (Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning) policy based on the Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) algorithm, with centralized training and decentralized execution, selects collision-aware navigation actions, while a high-rate Gerchberg-Saxton-Phased Array of Transducers (GS-PAT) acoustic controller maintains trap stability and updates array phases to achieve the commanded height during motion. This creates a closed perception-display-action loop. We evaluate single-robot city-to-city traversal and dual-robot cooperative coverage on a 4 m x 3 m scaled UK map using PhaseSpace-based localization for repeatable multi-robot trials. Results show stable in-motion levitation and consistent, location-dependent height rendering, with task success rates of 90% and 80% for the single and dual-robot regimes, respectively, over 10 trials per regime, and low collision counts. These findings support acoustophoretic levitation as a simple, glanceable, robot-mediated communication cue for embodied human-robot interaction in spatial analytics.

</details>


### [141] [Lift3D-VLA: Lifting VLA Models to 3D Geometry and Dynamics-Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06564)
*Jiaming Liu,Qingpo Wuwu,Nuowei Han,Hao Chen,Zhuoyang Liu,Fan Fei,Yueru Jia,Chenyang Gu,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lift3D-VLA提出统一框架，通过显式3D点云推理（几何对齐的2D模型提升策略+GC-MAE自监督学习）和分层时间动作建模，使VLA模型同时理解3D几何与动态时序，在22个模拟和8个真实操作任务上超越先前方法10.8%/11.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏显式3D几何推理能力，现有3D编码管线存在数据稀缺和几何信息丢失问题，无法在动态环境中联合捕捉3D几何与时序结构化动作。

Method: (1) 基于Lift3D的2D模型提升策略，几何对齐3D点与预训练2D位置嵌入，实现点云直接编码； (2) GC-MAE双目标自监督框架，同时重建当前点云并预测未来几何演化； (3) 分层时间动作建模，利用LLM多层协同预测动作块。

Result: MetaWorld和RLBench上平均成功率分别提升10.8%和11.1%，真实世界操作优于最强基线4个百分点，对分布外扰动有更强泛化。

Conclusion: 显式3D点云推理与几何自监督学习能显著增强VLA模型的3D时空推理能力，是实现通用机器人操作的关键方向。

Abstract: Recently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong generalization across diverse tasks. However, effective robotic manipulation in physical environments fundamentally requires geometric understanding and spatial reasoning. While some VLA approaches attempt to incorporate 3D information, they are constrained by limited data availability and geometric information loss in current 3D encoding pipelines, and fail to jointly capture 3D geometry and temporally structured actions in dynamic environments. To address these limitations, we introduce Lift3D-VLA, a unified VLA framework that equips models with explicit 3D point cloud reasoning and enables temporally coherent action generation. First, building upon our previous work Lift3D, an enhanced 2D model-lifting strategy is proposed to geometrically align 3D points with pretrained 2D positional embeddings. This design enables direct point-cloud encoding within the VLA vision encoder while minimizing spatial information loss. Based on explicit 3D inputs, we propose Geometry-Centric Masked Autoencoding (GC-MAE), a dual-objective self-supervised framework that reconstructs the current point cloud while predicting its future geometric evolution. This formulation allows the 2D vision encoder to internalize both 3D structure and physical dynamics. To fully exploit 3D representations, we further design layer-wise temporal action modeling, which leverages multiple layers of the LLM to collaboratively predict action chunks, enabling temporally consistent predictions. Across 22 simulated tasks and 8 real-world manipulation tasks, Lift3D-VLA achieves 10.8% and 11.1% higher mean success rates on MetaWorld and RLBench than the best-performing prior VLA methods, and outperforms the strongest real-world baseline by 4 percentage points, while exhibiting stronger generalization to out-of-distribution perturbations.

</details>
